| ★★★★★ | 지성 · 2020-09-12 | **So Far, So Good**<br>I only just recently started incorporating serums  into my skincare routine and found them to help keep my skin moisturized, smoother and more balanced. I also liked that the serum was vegan, clean and cruelty-free.  Unlike my other serums, the  main difference  I noticed immediately was it said in order to see optimal results, you would have to use it twice daily for four weeks. The first time I used this serum was in the morning, during my normal skin care routine. I used it after cleansing like the instructions said. I liked the texture and smell of this serum. The milky white consistency was not too thick and not too watery. The serum blended into the skin easily. There was a very, very, LIGHT fragrance that disappeared immediately after I applied it which I liked  despite not  liking fragranced products on my face. The serum dried slightly  tacky which I also liked. After it was completely dry I did notice a slight glow to my skin which I  really liked as well .Because I have oily skin, I usually am not a fan of products that  leave a glow or sheen-like effect, however I liked the fact that this serum was completely dry and my skin looked glowy versus actually being glowy.  The second day  I used this serum  I decided instead to use it after cleansing and moisturizing my face and used it in place of primer due to the tackiness. | 추가 정보 | With a blend of glycolic, tartaric, citric, and salicylic acids, T.L.C. Framboos&trade; delivers chemical exfoliation without dryness or sensitization, curbs excess oil, and reveals clarity and luminosity. It enhances the performance of other products by clearing the way for maximum absorption. pH 3.8.
<br><br> Drunk Elephant is clean, but in their opinion, &quot;clean&quot; is not enough when it comes to caring for skin. They make &quot;Clean Compatible&quot; skincare. Drunk Elephant is committed to using only ingredients that either directly benefit the skin’s health or support the integrity and effectiveness of their formulations. They never take into account an ingredient’s synthetic or natural status, but instead choose based on biocompatibility. You won’t find what they call the "Suspicious 6" in their products (essential oils, drying alcohols, silicones, chemical sunscreens, fragrances/dyes, and SLS) making them, in their opinion, appropriate for all skin. They believe that these six ubiquitous ingredients are at the root of almost every skin issue we see, and when entirely removed from one’s routine, (&quot;a clean break&quot;), skin can reset and return to a healthy, balanced state. It is equally, if not more, important what they leave out of the products as what they put in. This skin-elimination diet (think Whole30 for your face) inspired the #barewithus Instagram movement, a collection of bare-faced selfies chronicling the Drunk Elephant journeys of people who have found success using the line and renewed confidence in their own skin. When formulating their products, Drunk Elephant focuses on biocompatibility, pH levels, and safety, always making sure that the active ingredients are at efficacious levels and remain respectful and always supportive of skin’s acid mantle.<br><br>Drunk Elephant is a supporter of IEF (International Elephant Foundation) and is cruelty-free.<br><br>Award wins: 
<br>- StyleCaster, 2017 Award for 15 Best Skin Serums
<br>- New Beauty, 2017 Beauty Choice Award
<br>- Real Simple, 2018 Road Test Award | 글리콜산, 타르타르산, 시트릭산, 살리실산이 조합된 T.L.C. 프람부아™는 건조함이나 민감함 없이 화학적 각질 제거를 제공하며, 과도한 유분을 조절하고 맑고 빛나는 피부를 드러냅니다. 다른 제품의 흡수를 극대화할 수 있도록 피부를 정돈해줍니다. pH 3.8.<br><br>Drunk Elephant은 클린 뷰티 브랜드지만, 그들은 피부 관리에 있어 '클린'만으로는 충분하지 않다고 생각합니다. 그들은 '클린 호환성' 스킨케어를 만듭니다. Drunk Elephant은 피부 건강에 직접적으로 이롭거나 제형의 완성도와 효과를 지지하는 성분만을 사용합니다. 성분의 합성 또는 천연 여부는 고려하지 않고 생체 적합성을 기준으로 선택합니다. 그들은 '의심스러운 6가지'(에센셜 오일, 건조 알코올, 실리콘, 화학적 자외선 차단제, 향료/염료, SLS)를 제품에 포함하지 않아 모든 피부에 적합하다고 봅니다. 이 여섯 가지 성분이 거의 모든 피부 문제의 근원이라고 믿으며, 완전히 배제하면 ('클린 브레이크') 피부가 리셋되어 건강하고 균형 잡힌 상태로 돌아올 수 있다고 합니다. 제품에 넣는 성분만큼 빼는 성분도 중요합니다. 이 피부 제거 다이어트(얼굴을 위한 Whole30)에서 영감을 받은 #barewithus 인스타그램 운동은 Drunk Elephant 제품 사용 후 성공과 자신감을 찾은 사람들의 민낯 셀피 모음입니다. 제품을 개발할 때 Drunk Elephant은 생체 적합성, pH 수준, 안전성에 집중하며, 활성 성분이 효과적인 수준으로 유지되고 피부의 산성막을 존중하고 지지하도록 항상 신경 씁니다.<br><br>Drunk Elephant은 IEF(국제 코끼리 재단)를 후원하며 동물 실험을 하지 않습니다.<br><br>수상 내역:<br>- StyleCaster, 2017년 15대 베스트 스킨 세럼 상<br>- New Beauty, 2017 뷰티 초이스 상<br>- Real Simple, 2018 로드 테스트 상 |

| ★★★★ | 복합성 · 2021-07-28 | **Formula is different from my beloved older version**<br>I recently received a 7 day Clarins anti-aging duo sample from Influenster- the Double Serum & Extra-Firming Energy Day Cream. Clarins did what no other brand could for my skin, so I was hooked. Needless to say I was thrilled to give this #VoxBox my undivided attention for a week to test the Double Serum & Extra-Firming Energy Day Cream.DOUBLE SERUM* Two-phase formula that’s oil & water based. * 21  potent plant extracts {many of them organic}— including Banana, Oat, Teasal, Kiwi, Avocado, Goji Berry, Leaf of Life, Huang Qi, Turmeric, & such— to attack visible signs of aging.* Visibly firms, reduces wrinkles, smooths, hydrates, tightens pores, & boosts radiance in 7 days.* Eco-friendly bottle made from PCR & uses less packaging.* Serum boosts the performance of Clarins’ moisturizers.Seven days is not that long to test anti-aging skincare, but these are 2 that provide appreciable results in the short term, but even bigger, better ones 30+ days later. The drawbacks for me are price & that the Double Serum is not the same formula as the one I used & loved several years ago. <br>

<br>&quot;I’m inside most days. I don’t wear foundation, but I really wanted a little something I could mix into my other products to give my skin some color. The problem was I didn’t want to risk a breakout and I couldn’t find one that fit my suspicious six-free criteria. So, after a few attempts, we landed on a silky, natural-looking, bronzing concentrate. D-Bronzi&trade; is made of vitamins, antioxidants, platinum peptides, and barrier-supportive fatty acids that defend skin from environmental free radicals. It’s a gorgeous sheer bronzer that I formulated with skin health top-of-mind...like sunshine in a bottle without the damage. I hope you fall #drunkinlove!&quot;&#151;Tiffany Masterson, Brand Founder
<br><br>Drunk Elephant is committed to using only clean ingredients that either directly benefit the skin’s health or support the integrity and effectiveness of the formulations. They never take into account an ingredient’s synthetic or natural status, but instead choose based on its safety and bio-compatibility. You won’t find what Drunk Elephant calls &quot;the suspicious six&quot; of any kind in their products (1. silicones, 2. chemical screens, 3. sensitizing colorants/perfumes, 4. sodium lauryl sulfate (SLS) 5. essential oils, 6. drying alcohols), making them appropriate for all skin. They believe that these six ubiquitous ingredients are at the root of almost every skin issue. When formulating the products, they focus on pH and toxicity, always making sure that the actives are at efficacious levels. This is the Drunk Elephant difference.<br><br>Drunk Elephant is a supporter of IEF (International Elephant Foundation) and is cruelty-free.<br><br>Clean at Sephora<br> When you spot our Clean seal, you can be sure we’ve checked that this brand’s product is made without the ingredients you told us you’d most like to avoid.<br><br>Clean at Sephora&trade; is formulated without:<br>Sulfates SLS and SLES, parabens, formaldehydes, formaldehyde-releasing agents, phthalates, mineral oil, retinyl palmitate, oxybenzone, coal tar, hydroquinone, triclosan, triclocarban. <br>"저는 대부분 집 안에 있어요. 파운데이션은 안 바르는데, 피부에 색감을 줄 수 있는 뭔가를 다른 제품에 섞어 쓰고 싶었어요. 문제는 트러블 위험이 싫었고, 제가 의심하는 6가지 무첨가 기준에 맞는 제품을 못 찾았죠. 그래서 몇 번 시도 끝에 실키하고 자연스러운 브론징 농축액을 만들었어요. 제 화학자가 나중에 실험실에서 서로 하이파이브를 했다고 하더라고요. 실리콘 없이 이런 포뮬레이션을 만든 건 처음이라면서요. D- Bronzi™는 비타민, 항산화제, 백금 펩타이드, 장벽 강화 지방산으로 환경 유해 활성산소로부터 피부를 보호해요. 손상 없는 병 속 햇살 같은 고운 시어 브론저예요. 여러분도 #drunkinlove 되시길 바랄게요!"—티파니 마스터슨, 브랜드 창립자
<br><br>Drunk Elephant는 피부 건강에 직접 이롭거나 포뮬레이션의 완성도와 효과를 지지하는 깨끗한 성분만을 사용합니다. 성분의 합성 또는 천연 여부는 고려하지 않고 안전성과 생체 적합성에 따라 선택합니다. Drunk Elephant 제품에는 '의심스러운 6가지'(1. 실리콘, 2. 화학적 자외선 차단제, 3. 자극성 색소/향료, 4. 소듐라우릴설페이트(SLS), 5. 에센셜 오일, 6. 건조 알코올)가 전혀 포함되지 않아 모든 피부에 적합합니다. 이 6가지 성분이 거의 모든 피부 문제의 근원이라고 믿습니다. 제품 개발 시 pH와 독성을 중점으로 하며, 활성 성분은 효과적인 수준으로 유지합니다. 이것이 Drunk Elephant의 차별점입니다.<br><br>Drunk Elephant는 IEF(국제 코끼리 재단)를 후원하며 동물 실험을 하지 않습니다.<br><br>Clean at Sephora<br> Clean 마크가 붙은 제품은 고객님께서 피하고 싶어 하시는 성분이 포함되지 않았음을 보증합니다.<br><br>Clean at Sephora™는 다음 성분을 배제하여 제조됩니다:<br>황산염(SLS, SLES), 파라벤, 포름알데히드 및 방출제, 프탈레이트, 미네랄 오일, 레티닐 팔미테이트, 옥시벤존, 석탄 타르, 하이드로퀴논, 트리클로산, 트리클로카반. Clean Seal이 붙은 모든 스킨케어, 헤어, 메이크업 브랜드는 합성 향료가 1% 미만입니다. |

When formulating the products, they focus on pH levels and safety, and always making sure that the active ingredients are at efficacious levels. | 사용법 | -Use in the PM after cleansing and toning and before other serums.
<br>-Apply a pea-sized amount to the fingers and gently tap into the skin. 
<br><br>Precautions:<br>-Avoid the immediate eye area.
<br>-May increase sun sensitivity, always use a broad-spectrum sunscreen during the day.
<br>-Whilst The INKEY List Retinol has a low irritation formula, for some people, redness and flakiness may occur at the beginning of use due to the increase in cell turnover.
<br>-When first introducing retinol, you may wish to use a few times per week and gradually increase to every evening.
<br>-External use only. | - 저녁 세안 및 토닝 후, 다른 세럼 전에 사용하세요.

<br>- 햇빛에 대한 민감도가 증가할 수 있으니 낮에는 자외선 차단제를 꼭 사용하세요.

<br>- 외용으로만 사용하세요. |

| 제품 소개 | A cult-favorite retinol and AHA serum clinically shown to reduce the look of fine lines and wrinkles while visibly firming and brightening for glowing, younger-looking skin. | 잔주름과 주름 개선에 임상적으로 입증된 인기 레티놀 및 AHA 세럼으로, 탄력과 밝기를 눈에 띄게 개선하여 빛나고 젊어 보이는 피부로 가꿔줍니다. |
| 사용법 | -In the evening, smooth one to two pumps onto clean, dry skin.<br>-Start with one to two nights a week, add a night each week, building up to as often as your skin can tolerate.<br>-Follow with a moisturizer as needed.<br>-Be sure to use a sunscreen daily.<br>-Avoid using other AHA products on the same night as Retinol Reform.<br>-Do not use if pregnant or nursing. | - 저녁에 깨끗하고 건조한 피부에 1~2펌프를 부드럽게 펴 발라주세요.<br>- 처음에는 주 1~2회 사용 후 매주 1회씩 늘려 피부가 견딜 수 있는 만큼 사용하세요.<br>- 필요에 따라 보습제를 덧발라 주세요.<br>- 매일 자외선 차단제를 꼭 사용하세요.<br>- 레티놀 리폼과 같은 날 다른 AHA 제품 사용은 피하세요.<br>- 임신 중이거나 수유 중인 경우 사용하지 마세요. |
| 추가 정보 | Utilizing a time-release, encapsulated retinol, this formula delivers a consistent stream of retinol throughout the night to minimize irritation often experienced with retinol products. The formula also contains an AHA for smoothing and visible brightening benefits in as few as two weeks. This serum is clinically shown to improve texture in two weeks and reduce the look of fine lines and wrinkles in eight weeks. | 시간을 두고 서서히 방출되는 캡슐화 레티놀을 사용해 레티놀 제품 사용 시 흔히 겪는 자극을 최소화합니다. 또한 AHA가 포함되어 2주 만에 피부결 개선과 눈에 띄는 밝기 효과를 제공합니다. 이 세럼은 2주 만에 피부결을 개선하고 8주 만에 잔주름과 주름을 줄이는 임상 결과가 있습니다. |
| 피부 고민 | Fine Lines and Wrinkles, Dullness, Loss of Firmness and Elasticity | 잔주름·주름·칙칙함·탄력 저하 |
| 주요 성분 | · Encapsulated Retinol: Non-irritating retinol that improves the look of fine lines, texture, and elasticity.<br>· AHA: Exfoliates to improve texture and uneven tone for smoother, brighter-looking skin.<br>· Pyrus Malus (Apple) Fruit Extract: Antioxidant that helps reduce visible signs of aging and defend against pollution. | · 캡슐화된 레티놀: 자극 없이 잔주름, 피부결, 탄력 개선에 도움을 줍니다.<br>· AHA: 각질 제거로 피부결과 톤을 고르게 하여 더 부드럽고 밝은 피부로 가꿉니다.<br>· 사과(Pyrus Malus) 과일 추출물: 노화 징후 완화와 오염 방어에 도움을 주는 항산화 성분입니다. |

| 추가 정보 | Experience the science of retinol perfected with Retinol Tri-Active Technology, which delivers a dose of retinol over time. Formulated for all ages and skin types (including sensitive skin), this potent yet gentle serum quickly fights visible lines and wrinkles while increasing hydration for a plumper, firmer look. | 레티놀 트라이- 액티브 테크놀로지로 완성된 레티놀 과학을 경험하세요. 시간이 지나면서 레티놀을 서서히 전달하며, 민감한 피부를 포함한 모든 연령과 피부 타입에 맞게 개발된 이 강력하면서도 부드러운 세럼은 눈에 띄는 주름과 잔주름을 빠르게 개선하고, 수분감을 높여 더욱 탱탱하고 탄탄한 피부로 가꿔줍니다. |
